     1→# Implementer Subagent Prompt Template
     2→
     3→Use this template when dispatching an implementer subagent.
     4→
     5→```
     6→Task tool (general-purpose):
     7→  description: "Implement Task N: [task name]"
     8→  prompt: |
     9→    You are implementing Task N: [task name]
    10→
    11→    ## Task Description
    12→
    13→    [FULL TEXT of task from plan - paste it here, don't make subagent read file]
    14→
    15→    ## Context
    16→
    17→    [Scene-setting: where this fits, dependencies, architectural context]
    18→
    19→    ## Before You Begin
    20→
    21→    If you have questions about:
    22→    - The requirements or acceptance criteria
    23→    - The approach or implementation strategy
    24→    - Dependencies or assumptions
    25→    - Anything unclear in the task description
    26→
    27→    **Ask them now.** Raise any concerns before starting work.
    28→
    29→    ## Your Job
    30→
    31→    Once you're clear on requirements:
    32→    1. Implement exactly what the task specifies
    33→    2. Write tests (following TDD if task says to)
    34→    3. Verify implementation works
    35→    4. Commit your work
    36→    5. Self-review (see below)
    37→    6. Report back
    38→
    39→    Work from: [directory]
    40→
    41→    **While you work:** If you encounter something unexpected or unclear, **ask questions**.
    42→    It's always OK to pause and clarify. Don't guess or make assumptions.
    43→
    44→    ## Code Organization
    45→
    46→    You reason best about code you can hold in context at once, and your edits are more
    47→    reliable when files are focused. Keep this in mind:
    48→    - Follow the file structure defined in the plan
    49→    - Each file should have one clear responsibility with a well-defined interface
    50→    - If a file you're creating is growing beyond the plan's intent, stop and report
    51→      it as DONE_WITH_CONCERNS — don't split files on your own without plan guidance
    52→    - If an existing file you're modifying is already large or tangled, work carefully
    53→      and note it as a concern in your report
    54→    - In existing codebases, follow established patterns. Improve code you're touching
    55→      the way a good developer would, but don't restructure things outside your task.
    56→
    57→    ## When You're in Over Your Head
    58→
    59→    It is always OK to stop and say "this is too hard for me." Bad work is worse than
    60→    no work. You will not be penalized for escalating.
    61→
    62→    **STOP and escalate when:**
    63→    - The task requires architectural decisions with multiple valid approaches
    64→    - You need to understand code beyond what was provided and can't find clarity
    65→    - You feel uncertain about whether your approach is correct
    66→    - The task involves restructuring existing code in ways the plan didn't anticipate
    67→    - You've been reading file after file trying to understand the system without progress
    68→
    69→    **How to escalate:** Report back with status BLOCKED or NEEDS_CONTEXT. Describe
    70→    specifically what you're stuck on, what you've tried, and what kind of help you need.
    71→    The controller can provide more context, re-dispatch with a more capable model,
    72→    or break the task into smaller pieces.
    73→
    74→    ## Before Reporting Back: Self-Review
    75→
    76→    Review your work with fresh eyes. Ask yourself:
    77→
    78→    **Completeness:**
    79→    - Did I fully implement everything in the spec?
    80→    - Did I miss any requirements?
    81→    - Are there edge cases I didn't handle?
    82→
    83→    **Quality:**
    84→    - Is this my best work?
    85→    - Are names clear and accurate (match what things do, not how they work)?
    86→    - Is the code clean and maintainable?
    87→
    88→    **Discipline:**
    89→    - Did I avoid overbuilding (YAGNI)?
    90→    - Did I only build what was requested?
    91→    - Did I follow existing patterns in the codebase?
    92→
    93→    **Testing:**
    94→    - Do tests actually verify behavior (not just mock behavior)?
    95→    - Did I follow TDD if required?
    96→    - Are tests comprehensive?
    97→
    98→    If you find issues during self-review, fix them now before reporting.
    99→
   100→    ## Report Format
   101→
   102→    When done, report:
   103→    - **Status:** DONE | DONE_WITH_CONCERNS | BLOCKED | NEEDS_CONTEXT
   104→    - What you implemented (or what you attempted, if blocked)
   105→    - What you tested and test results
   106→    - Files changed
   107→    - Self-review findings (if any)
   108→    - Any issues or concerns
   109→
   110→    Use DONE_WITH_CONCERNS if you completed the work but have doubts about correctness.
   111→    Use BLOCKED if you cannot complete the task. Use NEEDS_CONTEXT if you need
   112→    information that wasn't provided. Never silently produce work you're unsure about.

\"Dashboard\",\n  \"nav.projects\": \"Projects\",\n  \"nav.todo\": \"Todo\",\n  \"nav.releases\": \"Releases\",\n  \"nav.channels\": \"Channels\",\n  \"nav.subscriptions\": \"Subscriptions\",\n \"Settings\"\n}\n```\n\n**Step 2:** Create Chinese messages file at `web/lib/i18n/messages/zh.json`:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"nav.dashboard\": \"仪表盘\",\n  \"nav.projects\": \"项目\",\n  \"nav.todo\": \"待办\",\n  \"nav.releases\": \"发布\",\n  \"nav.channels\": \"频道\",\n  \"nav.subscriptions\": \"订阅\",\n  \"settings.title\": \"设置\",\n  \"settings.theme\": \"主题\",\n  \"settings.theme.light\": \"浅色\",\n  \"settings.theme.dark\": \"深色\",\n  \"settings.theme.system\": \"跟随系统\",\n  \"settings.language\": \"语言\",\n  \"settings.language.en\": \"English\",\n  \"settings.language.zh\": \"中文\",\n  \"user.signout\": \"退出登录\",\n  \"user.settings\": \"设置\"\n}\n```\n\n**Step 3:** Create the LanguageProvider context at `web/lib/i18n/context.tsx`:\n\n```tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport {\n  createContext,\n  useContext,\n  useState,\n  useEffect,\n  useCallback,\n  type ReactNode,\n} from \"react\";\nimport en from \"./messages/en.json\";\nimport zh from \"./messages/zh.json\";\n\nconst messages: Record<string, Record<string, string>> = { en, zh };\n\ntype Locale = \"en\" | \"zh\";\n\ninterface I18nContextValue {\n  locale: Locale;\n  setLocale: (locale: Locale) => void;\n  t: (key: string) => string;\n}\n\nconst I18nContext = createContext<I18nContextValue | null>(null);\n\nconst STORAGE_KEY = \"changelogue-locale\";\n\nexport function LanguageProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  const [locale, setLocaleState] = useState<Locale>(\"en\");\n\n  // Read from localStorage on mount\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const stored = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n    if (stored === \"en\" || stored === \"zh\") {\n      setLocaleState(stored);\n      document.documentElement.lang = stored;\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  const setLocale = useCallback((next: Locale) => {\n    setLocaleState(next);\n    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, next);\n    document.documentElement.lang = next;\n  }, []);\n\n  const t = useCallback(\n    (key: string): string => {\n      return messages[locale]?.[key] ?? messages.en?.[key] ?? key;\n    },\n    [locale]\n  );\n\n  return (\n    <I18nContext.Provider value={{ locale, setLocale, t }}>\n      {children}\n    </I18nContext.Provider>\n  );\n}\n\nexport function useTranslation() {\n  const ctx = useContext(I18nContext);\n  if (!ctx) throw new Error(\"useTranslation must be used within LanguageProvider\");\n  return ctx;\n}\n```\n\n**Step 4:** Commit with message: `feat: add i18n context with EN/ZH translations`\n\n## Context\n\nThis is a Next.js 16 (App Router) project at `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon`. The `web/lib/` directory already exists with `auth/` and other utilities. You need to create `web/lib/i18n/` and its subdirectories.\n\nThe LanguageProvider stores the locale in localStorage key `\"changelogue-locale\"`, defaults to `\"en\"`, and syncs `document.documentElement.lang`.     39→export function Sidebar() {
    40→  const pathname = usePathname();

    45→
    46→  return (

    48→      className={cn(
    49→        "flex shrink-0 flex-col transition-[width] duration-200",

    51→      )}
    52→      style={{ backgroundColor: "var(--sidebar-bg)" }}
    53→    >
    54→      {/* Logo + toggle */}
    55→      <div className="flex h-12 items-center px-3">
    56→        {expanded ? (
    57→          <>
    58→            <img src="/logo.svg" alt="" className="h-7 w-7 shrink-0" />
    59→            <Link
    60→              href="/"
    61→              className="ml-2 text-[16px] italic text-white"
    62→              style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
    63→            >
    64→              Changelogue
    65→            </Link>
    66→            <button
    67→              onClick={() => setExpanded(false)}
    68→              className="ml-auto text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:text-white"
    69→              title="Collapse sidebar"
    70→            >
    71→              <PanelLeftClose className="h-4 w-4" />
    72→            </button>
    73→          </>
    74→        ) : (
    75→          <button
    76→            onClick={() => setExpanded(true)}
    77→            className="mx-auto text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:text-white"
    78→            title="Expand sidebar"
    79→          >
    80→            <PanelLeftOpen className="h-4 w-4" />
    81→          </button>
    82→        )}
    83→      </div>
    84→
    85→      {/* Nav */}
    86→      <nav className="flex-1 pt-2">
    87→        {navKeys.map((item) => {
    88→          const isActive =
    89→            item.href === "/"
    90→              ? pathname === "/"
    91→              : pathname.startsWith(item.href);
    92→          return (
    93→            <Link
    94→              key={item.href}
    95→              href={item.href}
    96→              title={expanded ? undefined : t(item.key)}
    97→              className={cn(
    98→                "flex items-center gap-3 py-2 text-[13px] transition-colors duration-150",
    99→                expanded ? "pl-4 pr-3" : "justify-center px-0",
   100→                isActive
   101→                  ? "text-white"
   102→                  : "text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-white"
   103→              )}
   104→              style={
   105→                isActive

   110→                    }
   111→                  : { borderLeft: "3px solid transparent" }
   112→              }
   113→            >
   114→              <item.icon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
   115→              {expanded && (
   116→                <span style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}>
   117→                  {t(item.key)}
   118→                </span>
   119→              )}
   120→            </Link>
   121→          );
   122→        })}
   123→      </nav>
   124→
   125→      {/* User section */}
   126→      {user && (
   127→        <div className="border-t border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.1)] p-2">
   128→          <DropdownMenu>
   129→            <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
   130→              <button
   131→                className={cn(
   132→                  "flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-md p-1 transition-colors hover:bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.06)]",
   133→                  expanded ? "px-2" : "justify-center"
   134→                )}
   135→              >

   138→                    src={user.avatar_url}
   139→                    alt={user.github_login}
   140→                    className="h-6 w-6 shrink-0 rounded-full"
   141→                  />
   142→                ) : (
   143→                  <div className="flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[var(--beacon-accent)] text-xs text-white">
   144→                    {user.github_login[0].toUpperCase()}
   145→                  </div>
   146→                )}
   147→                {expanded && (
   148→                  <span className="truncate text-xs text-[#9ca3af]">
   149→                    {user.github_login}
   150→                  </span>
   151→                )}
   152→              </button>
   153→            </DropdownMenuTrigger>
   154→            <DropdownMenuContent side="right" align="end" className="w-48">
   155→              <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => setSettingsOpen(true)}>
   156→                <Settings className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
   157→                {t("user.settings")}
   158→              </DropdownMenuItem>
   159→              <DropdownMenuSeparator />
   160→              <DropdownMenuItem onClick={logout}>

   162→                {t("user.signout")}

   164→            </DropdownMenuContent>
   165→          </DropdownMenu>
   166→          <SettingsDialog open={settingsOpen} onOpenChange={setSettingsOpen} />
   167→        </div>

## Summary Statistics

**Problem 1 - Dark Mode:**
- **150+** hardcoded color values found across the codebase
- **Critical impact:** Every component with a white/light background will be unreadable in dark mode
- **Files affected:** ~30 component and page files
- **Color patterns:** `#ffffff`, `#fafaf9`, `#f3f3f1`, `#e8e8e5`, `#111113`, `#6b7280`, `#9ca3af`, `#374151`, `#c4c4c0`, `#b0b0a8`, `#2563eb`, etc.

**Problem 2 - i18n:**
- **100+** hardcoded English user-facing strings
- **Critical impact:** All text visible to users is English-only
- **Files affected:** ~15 component and page files
- **Categories:** Page titles, labels, buttons, status messages, help text, all table headers, navigation labels

Both issues require systematic refactoring to use CSS variables for colors and i18n system (like `next-intl` or i18next) for text content.
